A dash means missing or insufficient history; zero means an observed zero.
Growth uses only one source, one observation per UTC day and a recent ~3-day regression; at least 18 hours of span is required.
Pace change compares that slope with the preceding, disjoint ~3-day window. It remains blank until both windows have enough evidence.
Imported baseline counts are retained for reference and excluded from exact growth.
Raw Watching + Completed before airing is not FAL scoring audience. The rules set pre-airing audience points to zero.

For each title, these are hypothetical individual points if that title were active; bench titles do not score while benched. The week ends at 22:00 UTC on the listed Sunday. A daily observation is held constant to show today's nowcast; it does not predict changes before cutoff. The rules count the anime's MAL mean score, not its number of scorers.

### Week 6 — cutoff 08 November 2026 22:00 UTC

Current hypothetical score **if these observed values held at the cutoff**. This is a nowcast, not a growth forecast or an official result.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.75; discussions × 75; MAL score inactive on (score − 6); dropped × inactive; MAL favorites × inactive.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.

### Episode discussion audit

Counts are observed so far, not a forecast of posts still to come. Week 2 includes all posts up to the observation/cutoff. Later even weeks use the previous two weeks; Week 13 uses one week. Discovery uses the anime forum independently of its Episodes list, with exact-title official forum search for your roster and missing lists. “Usable” means all discovered threads were paginated; it is not proof that MAL/FAL has registered every thread. Forum-search-only threads have an unconfirmed anime/FAL association; their points are excluded from the main total and shown as a separate scenario. Cached discovery can miss a newly created Sunday thread. Recheck near cutoff and inspect links where coverage is uncertain.

## Completed-week observation checkpoints

Each past week uses the earliest MAL observation within 24 hours after its cutoff, or the latest within 24 hours before it if no post-cutoff observation exists. The observation time and minute offset appear per title. Counts are not exact 22:00 UTC values; unknown components mean the known subtotal is not an official final score.

### Week 1 — cutoff 04 October 2026 22:00 UTC

Archived observation near the cutoff; **not an official locked FAL result**. The displayed per-title observations can precede or follow the cutoff.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.5; discussions × inactive; MAL score inactive on (score − 6); dropped × inactive; MAL favorites × inactive.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.

### Week 2 — cutoff 11 October 2026 22:00 UTC

Archived observation near the cutoff; **not an official locked FAL result**. The displayed per-title observations can precede or follow the cutoff.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.75; discussions × 75; MAL score inactive on (score − 6); dropped × inactive; MAL favorites × inactive.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.

### Week 3 — cutoff 18 October 2026 22:00 UTC

Archived observation near the cutoff; **not an official locked FAL result**. The displayed per-title observations can precede or follow the cutoff.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.5; discussions × inactive; MAL score × 17,500 on (score − 6); dropped × inactive; MAL favorites × inactive.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.

### Week 4 — cutoff 25 October 2026 22:00 UTC

Archived observation near the cutoff; **not an official locked FAL result**. The displayed per-title observations can precede or follow the cutoff.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.75; discussions × 75; MAL score inactive on (score − 6); dropped × -4; MAL favorites × inactive.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.

### Week 5 — cutoff 01 November 2026 22:00 UTC

Archived observation near the cutoff; **not an official locked FAL result**. The displayed per-title observations can precede or follow the cutoff.

Rates: Watching + Completed × 0.5; discussions × inactive; MAL score inactive on (score − 6); dropped × inactive; MAL favorites × 15.

Known subtotal excludes missing inputs, Ace and wildcard. An absent score, discussion count or favorite count is **unknown**, not zero. Discussion counts deduplicate posters within each episode thread, then sum across threads. Partial counts are excluded from the known subtotal. Poll votes are excluded. The discussion-inclusive estimate uses complete counted threads even when their FAL association is unconfirmed; it is a conditional estimate, not an official score. Missing/unconfirmed can describe attribution rather than absent counts.
